本次報告：座談會

南亞 (1303 TT) 
不再只是塑膠，電子材料成長動能強勁

永豐觀點 
南亞 1Q26 獲利優於預期。受惠 AI 應用蓬勃發展，電子材料成長動能強勁，且業
外轉投資南亞科、台塑化獲利增長，將挹注南亞獲利表現，研究部看好南亞 2026
年營運展望正向，上調目標價，給予買進投資建議。 
 投資評價與建議 
投資建議給予買進 ，主要係因：受惠 AI 需求持續成長，中高階材料需求大幅增加，
電子材料產品營收佔比已超過 50%，且公司看好未來將持續提升至 60%，電子材
料事業預期在 2026 年將迎來大幅度成長，而公司業外受惠轉投資台塑化、南亞科
獲利成長，將挹注大幅轉投資獲利貢獻，基此，研究部預估 2026 稅後 EPS 10.77
元，因南亞電子材料營收比重已超過 50%，且公司持續正向看待未來將成長至
60%，故研究部以本益比進行評價，參考同業及歷史本益比區間，給予 11X 本益
比評價，對應 2.3X 本淨比評價，目標價上調至 118 元，投資建議買進。

### Page 2

2

南亞為電子材料、塑膠、化工及聚酯產品生產廠商：南亞成立於 1958 年，為全球
最大塑膠二次加工製造商，電子級玻纖絲製造為世界第二大，銅箔基板、玻纖布為
世界第三大，乙二醇（EG）為世界第四大，環氧樹脂、丙二酚（BPA）為世界第五
大。南亞營收分為四大部門，包括塑膠加工產品、化工產品、電子材料產品、聚酯
製品，公司 2025 產品營收比重為電子材料占 46%（台灣占 43%，中國占 57%），
化工產品占 22%（台灣占 58%，美國占 27%，中國占 15%） ，聚酯產品占16%
（台灣占 39%，美國占 56%，中國占 5%） ，塑膠加工產品占14%（台灣占 61%，
美國占 15%，中國占 24%） ，其他占2%。 
1Q26 稅後 EPS 為 1.8 元，優於預期：南亞 1Q26 營收 685.96 億元，+6.4％ 
QoQ，+4.6% YoY，營收優於預期，1Q26 營收較 4Q25 增加 41.3 億元，其中，
因銷量增加影響營收季增 1.3 億元，因售價上漲影響營收季增 40 億元，主要係因
電子材料產品需求強勁，銷售量價齊揚，帶動營收顯著成長，毛利率 11.94%，
+2.69pts QoQ，+4.89pts YoY，優於預期，主因電子材料產品出貨營收增長，包
含 ABF 載板、玻纖絲、玻纖布、銅箔、CCL 皆有顯著貢獻，產品組合優化所致，
稅後淨利 142.55 億元，+189.4% QoQ，+3045.97% YoY，其中，1Q26 業外權
益法投資認列收益 121.2 億元，較上季有利 82 億元，優於預期，主要包含台塑化
認列 47.1 億元 （影響公司EPS 0.59 元） ，較上季有利35.3 億元，南亞科認列76.3
億元（影響公司 EPS 0.96 元） ，較上季有利43.8 億元，1Q26 稅後 EPS 1.8 元，優
於預期。 
2Q26 獲利預期將季增 ：南亞 4 月營收 276.8 億元 （+1.9％ MoM，+19.4% YoY），
優於預期，主因電子材料需求暢旺，售價依市況調漲，且 CCL、ABF 載板、玻纖
布、玻纖絲等產品銷售動能依然強勁，價量持續放大所致。展望2Q26，電子材料
需求暢旺，公司目前環氧樹脂、玻纖絲、玻纖布產能近滿載，銅箔、CCL 產能利用
率則達 80-90%，電子材料營收佔比預期將持續增加；化工部門目前以銷售庫存為
主，麥寮 EG 產線皆暫停運轉，德州 EG 產線則維持全能生產，預期營收將季增；

營運現況與分析 
表一：南亞營收結構

資料來源：公司簡報；永豐投顧研究部整理，Jun. 2026 
表二：南亞各產品營業利益率變化

資料來源：公司簡報；永豐投顧研究部整理，Jun. 2026

### Page 3

3

聚酯產品則因報價調漲，客戶趨於保守下單，預期營收將略增；塑膠加工產品營收
預期將持平 ， 整體而言 ， 因電子材料營收佔比預期提升 ， 且公司因應不同客戶訂單 ，
穩定調漲電子材料售價，產品組合將持續優化，帶動南亞獲利表現成長，研究部預
估 2Q26 營收 812.86 億元，（+18.5％ QoQ，+23.69％ YoY） ，預估毛利率
15.62%，（+3.68 pts. QoQ，+6.98 pts. YoY） ， 預估稅後淨利201.7 億元（+41.5% 
QoQ，轉虧為盈 YoY） ，稅後EPS 2.54 元。 
2026 電子材料營收大幅成長，給予買進投資建議：展望 2026 年，受惠 AI 需求持
續成長，電子材料產品營收佔比已超過 50%，因產品量價齊揚，產品營收佔比穩
定成長中，公司目前正持續發展 CCL 產品，預期 M8、M9 系列在通過認證後，將
於 2H26 逐步放量生產，M10 系列則持續認證開發中，其餘電子材料產品，受惠
AI 對於中高階材料需求大幅增加，造成中低階、一般電子級材料供給趨緊，銷售
量價齊揚，公司也積極爭取中高階材料訂單，南亞電子材料事業預期在2026 年將
迎來大幅度成長。而公司業外受惠轉投資台塑化、南亞科獲利成長，將挹注大幅轉
投資獲利貢獻 ， 其中 ， 預估南亞科貢獻南亞EPS 可達 7.5 元 。 綜合以上 ， 南亞2026
年電子材料產品展望正向，將填補其餘目前獲利狀況較差的化工、聚酯部門。南亞
先前公告將處分 30,986 張的南亞科及 38,770 張的南電，其中，因南亞是以權益
法持有南亞科，處分南亞科應認列業外獲利，因南亞尚未公告處分完畢，故研究部
不納入此次獲利預估，基此，研究部預估 2026 年營收 3,228.73 億元（+24.22％ 
YoY），毛利率 15.16%，稅後淨利 853.82 億元（+1789.57％YoY），稅後 EPS 
10.77 元，因南亞電子材料營收比重已超過 50%，且公司持續正向看待未來將成
長至 60%，故研究部以本益比進行評價，參考同業及歷史本益比區間，給予 11X
本益比評價，對應 2.3X 本淨比評價，目標價上調至 118 元，投資建議買進

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

1 
1736 喬山(Johnson Health)

報告日期： 2026/06/02 研 究 員：洪  國 峰 (Titan Horng)  
壹、投 資 建 議

一. 公告 1Q26 稅後 EPS 0.08 元 
喬山公告 1Q26 營收金額 117.92 億元， YoY+11.15% ，
QoQ-39.13% ，營業利益 -0.93 億元， YoY-206.5% ，
QoQ-104.89% ， 稅後盈餘0.24 億元 ，YoY-91.34% ，QoQ-98.6% ，
稅後 EPS 0.08 元。

二. 預估 2Q26 稅後 EPS 1.98 元 
喬山 04/2026 營收金額 39.98 億元， YoY+6.4% ，MoM-6.31% 。
預估喬山 2Q26 營收金額 133.03 億元， YoY+8.1% ，
QoQ+12.81% ， 預估營業利益10.96 億元 ，YoY+31.29% ，較 1Q26
由虧轉盈，預估稅後盈餘 6.00 億元， YoY+436.25% ，
QoQ+2409% ，預估稅後 EPS 1.98 元。

三 . 預估 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元，
YoY+15.98%  
商用市場 1Q26 營收金額 2.35 億美元， YoY+17.21% 。商用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括 ：1， 在歐美健身房主要客戶滲透率持續
提升，另外也拓展歐美各國本土前五大健身房客戶。 2，產品線擴
展，增加高階健身器材產品線，以便拓展高端健身房市場，增加新
客群。 3，商用健身器材零組件主要以台灣與中國上海為主，預期
在越南工廠生產比重提升帶動下，將有助整體毛利率提升。 預估喬
山 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98% 。

四. 預估 2026 年稅後 EPS 13.00 元 
喬山 2026 年在歐美健身房客戶持續在海外設據點 ， 加上客戶轉單
效益 ， 搭配旗下越南廠生產效率提升與產能擴充 ， 將有助喬山2026
年營運持續成長。宏遠預估喬山 2026 年營收金額 630.00 億元，
YoY+15.96% ，預估營業利益 59.86 億元， YoY+66.66% ，預估稅
後盈餘 39.44 億元， YoY+43.43% ，預估稅後 EPS 13.00 元。

五. 投資建議買進，目標價 156 元 
觀察喬山歷史 PER 落在 8~33X，2026 年 ROE 26.7%，PER 9.1X，
評價在低檔水準，加上 2026 年營運成長，故投資建議買進，目標
價 156 元。 (12X 2026 年稅後 EPS) 
投資評等：買進

2

貳、公 司 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
喬山健康科技創立於 1975 年，資本額 30.34 億元，並於 2003 年
掛牌上市，喬山為亞洲第一、世界前三大的國際專業運動健身器
材集團公司，產品包括電動跑步機、室內健身車、橢圓訓練機、
重量訓練器材、划船器材、電動按摩椅、心肺訓練器材等。喬山
以「健康、價值、共享」為企業經營理念，專注於健康科技事業
的發展，並以 Matrix 、Vision 和 Horizon 自有品牌行銷全世界 80
餘國。生產基地位於台中、上海、與美國越南，其中台中生產高
單價的商用產品、上海主要生產製造量大的家用產品與小部分商
用產品、美國主要生產製造特殊重量訓練產品、越南主要生產製
造家用產品為主。

二.品牌定位與銷售通路  
喬山旗下擁有 Matrix 、Vision 和 Horizon 三項自有品牌，其中
Martrix 主打高價位商用市場 ， 其主要銷售對象包括健身房 、 飯店、
運動員訓練中心、大型公司、運動專賣店等； Vision 主打中高價
位的市場，主要銷售對象以大型公司、運動專賣店為主； Horizon
主打家用市場為主，銷售對象包括運動專賣店、電商與大型運動
器材專賣店。

三. 2026 年營運概況及展望

全球健身器材市場 100 億美元 (宏遠投顧推估 )，其中商用市場 50
億美元、家用市場 50 億美元。觀察全球主要廠商 2024 年營收金
額概況： Life 6.75 億美元， YoY+4.2%( 宏遠投顧推估 )；TG 7.67
億美元 ，YoY+13.6%；喬 山10.39 億美元 ，YoY+18.3%；Precor2.61
億美元， YoY+26.1% ；Pelonton 27.0 億美元， YoY-1.1% 。

預估 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98%

喬山商用市場客戶包括 ： 健身房北美星球健身 、 歐洲BASIC-FIT 、
拉丁美洲 Smart Fit 以及全球連鎖性健身房 Anytime Finess 、台 灣
World GYM 與健身工廠等；飯店客戶 Marriott 、STARWOOD 、
FOUR SEASONS 等。

圖五、喬山健身房客戶

資料來源：喬山 &宏遠投顧彙整

商用市場 2025 年營收金額 12.33 億美元， YoY+18.67% 。觀察喬
山商用市場 2025 年營運成長動能包括： 1，在中國大陸健身器材
出口至美國課徵高額關稅 ， 相對於台灣與越南關稅較低，帶動健身
房客戶訂單轉向台灣與越南地區。 2，歐洲大型客戶持續拓展美國
市場，另外美國主要客戶也拓展歐洲市場，歐洲與美國主要客戶
2025 年分別成長 25~28% 與 21~24% 。3，歐洲地區受惠中小型客
戶需求增溫，帶動歐洲地成長 25~30% 。

商用市場 1Q26 營收金額 2.35 億美元， YoY+17.21% 。商用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括 ：1， 在歐美健身房主要客戶滲透率持續
提升，另外也拓展歐美各國本土前五大健身房客戶。 2，產品線擴
展，增加高階健身器材產品線，以便拓展高端健身房市場，增加新
客群。 3，商用健身器材零組件主要以台灣與中國上海為主，預期
在越南工廠生產比重提升帶動下，將有助整體毛利率提升。 預估喬
山 2026 年商用市場營收金額 14.3 億美元， YoY+15.98% 。

圖六、擴展商用市場新產品 /新通路

資料來源：喬山 &宏遠投顧彙整

預估 2026 年家用市場營收金額 4.00 億美元， YoY+12.68%

喬山家用市場主要以運動專賣店、電商與大型運動器材專賣店為
主。喬山為拓展家用市場零售通路，陸續在 2015 年~2016 年購併
2nd Wind 、BUSY BODY 、Leisure Fitness 與 Gym Source 等健
身器材銷售通路，喬山在北美銷售通路據點共 103 家，據點數占
北美 30% 。

家用市場 2025 年營收金額 3.55 億美元 ，YoY+17.16% 。喬 山2025
年家用營運成長主要包括： 1，BowFlex 在 2025 年貢獻營收金額
1.1~1.2 億美元， YoY+53~67% 。2，觀察歐洲地區與拉丁美洲受
惠家用健身器材營運基期較低，商用健身房興起帶動家用市場需
求熱絡，帶動歐洲地區與拉丁美洲家用市場分別成長 45~48% 與
22~25% 。3，觀察全球主要家用健身器材廠商，生產基地集中在
中國大陸，在美國對中國大陸出口產品課徵高額關稅因素，許多
訂單己移至越南生產，帶動喬山越南廠稼動率提升。

家用市場 1Q26 營收金額 1.01 億美元， YoY+12.85% 。家用市場
2026 年營運成長動能包括： 1，家用健身器材經歷 2020~2021 年
上半年疫情期間大好， 2021 下半年至 2023 年庫存調整， 2025 年
因關稅訂單偏保守，觀察零售通路庫存已達相對低檔水準，加上
己有廠商退出市場，將有助家用市場成長。 2，喬山持續拓展零售
據點效益顯現，例如大型量販店己向喬山採購多項家用產品線，
帶動大型量販通路營收。 3，家用旗下 BowFlex 在 2026 年推出新
產品，搶攻北美家用市場，將有助 BowFlex  2026 年出貨成長。
綜合上述，預估 2025 年喬山家用市場營收金額 4.00 億美元，
YoY+12.68% 。

預估富士醫療 (含自有品牌按摩椅 )貢獻 2026 年營收金額 1.70 億
美元， YoY+6.9%

喬山 10/29/2019 公告，以 67 億日幣 （約當台幣20 億元），向「朝
日生活與健康股份有限公司 Asahi Life & Health Corporation 」買
下子公司「富士醫療器股份有限公司 FUJI MEDICAL 
INSTRUMENT 」 60% 股權，「富士醫療器股份有限公司 FUJI 
MEDICAL INSTRUMENT 」為日本最大按摩椅製造商，擁有業界
領先產品開發和品牌實力，另外在日本佈有綿密的銷售通路，觀
察富士醫療器年營收金額 180~200 億日幣 (約當美元 1.8~2 億美
元)，己於 03/2020 完成股權交割。喬山 12/09/2022 公告取得富
士醫療器 40% 股權 ， 交易完成後喬山持有富士醫療器100% 股權。

摩椅 2025 年營收金額 0.31 億美元， YoY+6.9% 。觀察富士醫療營
收成長主因在於富士醫療拓展日本以外的外銷市場有成。

YoY+9.09% 預期 2026 年，在富士醫療持續拓展日本以外的外銷
市場，加上喬山自有品牌按摩椅穩定成長。預估富士醫療 2026 年
營收金額 1.36 億美元， YoY+6.25% ；預估喬山自有品牌按摩椅
2026 年營收金額 0.34 億元 ，YoY+9.68%， 合計兩者貢獻喬山2026
年營收金額 1.70 億美元， YoY+6.9% 。

元

喬山 2026 年在歐美健身房客戶持續在海外設據點 ， 加上客戶轉單
效益 ， 搭配旗下越南廠生產效率提升與產能擴充 ， 將有助喬山2026
年營運持續成長。宏遠預估喬山 2026 年營收金額 630.00 億元，
YoY+15.96% ，預估營業利益 59.86 億元， YoY+66.66% ，預估稅
後盈餘 39.44 億元， YoY+43.43% ，預估稅後 EPS 13.00 元。

公告配發現金股利 4.5 元，現金殖利率 3.8%

利政策，配發現金股利 4 元，股利發放率 50% 。喬山公告 2025
年股利政策，將配發現金股利 4.5 元，現金殖利率 3.8% 。

- 1 - 
個股報告 
國巨(2327) 
高階 MLCC 供需緊俏，價格調漲與產能排擠效應帶動獲利
續升 
投資評等 目標價 
逢低買進 $1020 
前次投資建議：2026.04.16 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價: $384

報告日期：2026.06.04 研 究 員：李彝安

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ 研究部將國巨目標價由 384 元調升至 1,020元（35x 28(F) PER），潛在上漲空間 24.4%。評
價基礎由 26F移至28F， 反映本輪被動元件漲價 、 稼動率回升與費用槓桿效益可望延續至2027
至 2028 年。投資評等維持「逢低買進」 。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/06/03 收盤價： 820.0 ➢ 國巨 26Q1 營運延續成長，26Q2 稼動率高於季初指
引。26Q1 營收 381.7 億元，QoQ +6.1%、YoY +22.7%，
毛利率 38.1%，EPS 3.90 元。26Q2 標準品與高階品
稼動率均高於季初法說指引，顯示需求回補已由鉭電容
延伸至 MLCC、電阻與電感等主要產品線。 
➢ Rubin平台升級推升 MLCC用量， 日韓產能排擠使國巨
受惠。VR200單機 MLCC美元含量與顆數均較 GB300
明顯增加，高容值 MLCC 佔比同步提升。日韓廠產能
優先配置高階 AI產品後，一般 IT 與中低階元件供給轉
緊。國巨在標準品、中高階 MLCC 及一般 IT 應用布局
完整，受惠重點在稼動率提升與報價環境改善。 
➢ 鉭電容漲價趨勢延續，提供非 MLCC 獲利支撐。國巨
透過 KEMET取得鉭質電容領先地位，AI 伺服器需求使
鉭電容訂單能見度延伸。鉭原料成本上行亦提供報價調
整基礎。 
➢ 預估 2026/27/28 年 EPS 為 17.38/23.80/29.18 元、
2026/27/28年營收 1,667.58（YoY +25.45%）、2,004.02
（YoY +20.18%）、2,382.22 億元（YoY +18.87%） 。受
惠 AI 需求帶動高階 MLCC/鉭電容供給趨緊、價格調
漲，以及產能排擠效應延續，研究部上修2026 至 2028
年獲利預估，並將預估此循環將延續至 2028F。考慮同
業 PER 評價區間及國巨產業定位，目標價 1,020 元
（35x 28(F) PER） ， 潛在上漲空間24.4%， 評等維持 「逢
低買進」 。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐國際預估

營 運 分 析 
國巨為全球被動元件龍頭，併購驅動一站式解決方案平台 
國巨股份有限公司成立於 1977 年，是全球領先的被動元件製造商，產品涵
蓋晶片電阻、積層陶瓷電容（MLCC）、鉭質電容、電感、電解電容、高頻
天線與感測器等，廣泛應用於車用電子、工業控制、通訊設備與 AI 伺服器
領域。目前國巨為全球最大晶片電阻與鉭質電容供應商，並居全球第三大  
MLCC 與電感製造商，擁有完整的產品組合與全球製造據點。 
近年來，國巨積極 透過海外併購與策略性投資擴大營運版圖，包含併購  
KEMET、Pulse、君耀及奇力新等廠商，跨足類比 IC 與功率元件領域。2025 
年 Q4 進一步完成收購日本 Shibaura（全球熱敏電阻市佔第一），結合先
前併入的 Nexensos（PTC 溫度感測器），在感測器領域形成完整產品線。 
Q1產品營收結構為磁性元件 25.2%、鉭質電容 24.3%、MLCC 19.0%、電
阻 13.5%、感測器 13.0%、其他 5.0%；25 年終端應用以工業 28.9% 佔
比最高，其次為電腦及企業系統 25.2%、車用 20.1%、消費電子 12.2%、
電信 10.3%、航太軍工醫療 3.4%。產品組合中高階品佔比約  75%、標準
品約 25%。區域別方面，美國 30.4%、亞洲 （不含中國）26.0%、中國 23.0%、
歐洲 20.5%；通路結構為經銷商 39.9%、OEM 37.8%、EMS 22.3%。 
圖一、國巨(2327) 2026Q1產品別營收比重。

資料來源：國巨，兆豐國際彙整

- 3 - 
個股報告 
Rubin平台升級推升 MLCC用量，國巨受惠供給排擠效應 
AI伺服器平台由 GB300進入 Rubin VR200後，單機架 MLCC用量提升 80%
與美元含量明顯提升 1.8 倍。量增主要來自 compute board 與 switch board，
新一代模組可望帶來的額外用量。更重要的是規格組合改變，高容值 MLCC
佔比提升後，將占用日韓廠較多高階產能資源。 
國巨並非高容值 AI MLCC的主要領導者 ， 這點反而使本輪受惠邏輯與日韓廠
不同。日韓廠直接承接高階AI MLCC需求，國巨則受惠其產能被排擠後，提
供一站式一般 IT、標準品與中高階 MLCC/被動元件解決方案。在消費性電子
衰退預期下，此優勢在同業中隨稼動率提升與交期拉長，國巨的議價環境轉
佳。 
與 2018 年不同，目前市場未見恐慌性囤貨的跡象。前次報告已提到，經銷
商 months on hand 已較一年前下降，且客戶以穩定供應為前提接受調價，
而非競相囤貨。這使本輪價格調整較接近供需正常化後的毛利修復，而不是
單純庫存循環。

鉭電容漲價趨勢延續，非 MLCC產品提高獲利穩定性 
鉭質電容仍為國巨本輪獲利上修的重要來源。AI伺服器功率密度提升，板面
空間受限，使鉭質電容在電源管理與高可靠度應用中的重要性提高。國巨透
過 KEMET 取得鉭電容領先地位，具備較高定價能力，且鉭電容訂單能見度
已延伸至下半年。 
研究部將鉭電容視為長期受需求成長、成本上升與供給結構影響的產品線。
原料成本上升有助於支撐價格調整，AI相關應用需求增加則帶動終端需求。
若後續價格調整順利反映成本變化，鉭電容獲利能力仍有進一步改善空間。 
除鉭電容外，電阻與電感亦為後續獲利支撐。晶片電阻用量與MLCC具高度
連動，國巨具備高市占與成本優勢；電感則受惠 AI伺服器 power module、
車用與電信應用需求。非MLCC產品線同步回升，可降低市場對單一MLCC
循環的疑慮。

獲利預估上修反映費用槓桿與營運效率改善 
前次報告已反映 26Q1 財報優於研究部原先模型、鉭電容漲價分批進入損益
表，以及通路庫存回到健康水位，但對費用槓桿估計偏保守。當時模型較接
近營收、毛利與費用同步上調的假設，因此低估營收上修後對營業利益率的
貢獻。 
本輪營收增量主要來自價格調整、稼動率提升與產品組合改善，並不需要等
比例增加銷售、管理與研發費用。換言之，若同樣增加 100 元營收，本輪有
較高比例可留在營業利益。研究部預估營業費用率由 26F 的 12.1%下降至
28F 的 9.4%，營業利益率由 27.1%提升至 32.4%。這是造成先前 EPS與目
標價低估的主因。

- 4 - 
個股報告 
圖二、國巨(2327)各季營收及獲利走勢預估

資料來源：Cmoney；兆豐國際預估

獲利預估上修，費用槓桿支撐評價調升 
研究部預估國巨 2026 年營收 1,667.6 億元，YoY +25.4%，毛利率 39.2%，
稅後淨利 360.2 億元，EPS 17.4元；2027 年營收 2,004.0 億元，YoY 
+20.2%，毛利率 41.2%，稅後淨利 493.5 億元，EPS 23.8元；2028年營收
2,382.2 億元，YoY +18.9%，毛利率41.8%，稅後淨利605.0 億元，EPS 29.2
元。 
本次上修是重新評估 2026 至 2028 年價格調整、產能排擠與費用槓桿對損益
表的影響。毛利率提升來自價格調整、稼動率回升及高階品占比提升；營業
利益率提升則來自費用率下降。估值方面，評價基礎由 26F 移至 28F，反映
本輪被動元件循環並非單季或單年度事件，上調目標價 1,020元（35x 28(F) 
PER），潛在上漲空間 24.4%，投資評等維持「逢低買進」。

- 5 -

股價相對大盤走勢

創見 (2451 TT)  Trading Buy

後續毛利率擴張有限 ， 下調至Trading Buy ，目 標 價393 元。 
投資建議 ： AI 需求佳，大幅提升記憶體需求，目前原廠供應的
合約滿足率低於 50% ，創見雖然也與原廠簽訂長期供貨合約
（ LTA ） ，但原廠仍是依照既定配額供貨，創見目前庫存水位約
100 多億元， 2Q26 庫存金額還會增加，但主要是單價上升，實
際數量變化不大。目前 DRAM 與 Flash 供給都相當吃緊，雖 3
大原廠已投入大量資本支出擴產，但最快也要到 2027 年才陸續
開出新產能，推升報價進一步上揚，預估創見 2026 年稅後 EPS 
106.10 元。創見公告 05/2026 合併營收達 62.98 億元， MoM-
15.46% 、YoY+469.83% ；累計 04/2026~05/2026 營收達 137.5 億
元，高於 1Q26 整季營收 136.3 億元。記憶體報價仍處上升趨
勢，支撐獲利成長，惟隨市場預期價格漲勢趨緩，毛利率擴張
空間有限，因此下調至 Trading Buy ，目標價 393 元。  
1Q26 稅後 EPS 18.86 元：因 AI 需求爆發引發結構性缺貨，記
憶體供不應求，在工控市場剛性需求帶動下，創見 1Q26 營收為
136.28 億元， QoQ+100.31% ，毛利率  76.39% ，季增 14.77 個百
分點，稅後純益  81.22 億元， QoQ+148.95% ，稅後 EPS 18.86
元。毛利率高於同業主因在於自動化與生產效能優勢，及低成
本結構，包括許多廠房已使用 20、 30 年，折舊接近完成，全球
據點也多為自有廠房，因此整體營運成本低於同業。  
創見工業用佔比 70% 以上： 創見 2026 年初工控相關應用營收
佔比已升至  70% 以上， 2Q26 占營收比重增加至 90% ，主要是
價格高，消費性通路銷售明顯下滑。創見工控以高階嵌入式市
場為主，涵蓋工業用 SSD 、 Flash 、記憶卡與  DRAM 模組，廣
泛應用於 AIoT 、醫療、交通及強固型電腦。創見在記憶體上游
原廠宣布產品 EOL 之前，已提前 2 年鎖定採購量與長約配額，
讓公司在市場面臨 DRAM 和 NAND Flash 舊世代缺料潮時，能
確保穩定的出貨能力。因工控產品需長期穩定供貨，且為客製
化需求，毛利率較高，是創見撐住缺貨潮、達成高獲利的主力。

2 群益投顧 
  創見 ESG ：  
創見官網將  ESG 分別對應環境 （永續環境） 、社會 （社會公益） 與治理 （公
司治理） ，並於 2025 年成立永續發展委員會來推動，涵蓋政策規劃、成效
檢視與跨部門執行。  近期成效包括修訂 《永續發展實務守則》 、納入  TCFD 
氣候風險評估，以及持續發行年度永續報告書。  
E：環境  
創見強調廢棄物管理、節能措施、有害物質審查與低碳轉型。官網寫到公司
投入環保產品開發，並響應氣候變遷規畫減碳政策，透過評估風險與機會來
降低能源消耗與碳排放。  
S：社會  
社會面聚焦維護社會公益與利害關係人權益。官網依《永續發展實務守則》
承諾善盡責任，透過永續發展委員會的社會公益小組推動相關活動。  
G：治理  
治理面由行政副總經理任永續負責人，設立公司治理小組處理政策與資訊
揭露。官網強調董事會監督、跨部門協作，並將  ESG 納入管理方針。

  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

Hon Hai (2317.TW): Computex 2026: AI servers platform capabilities
showcased; New AI infrastructure projects in Europe; Buy (on CL)
2 June 2026 | 10:53PM HKT
We visited the Hon Hai booth at Computex on June 2, where the company
showcased its AI servers and high-speed interconnect solution in various
architecture, optical products (e.g. ELSFP for CPO switch, 1.6T optical modules),
Space data center solution, and AI models/ Agents system. The company

demonstrated its comprehensive product offerings, manuf acturing, and system
integration capabilities for AI infrastructure, and also highlighted its AI server
deployment through local partnerships (for example, new AI data center projects in
Europe). We remain positive on Hon Hai, given its strong R&D capabilities, global
supply chain with scale advantages, and migration to the full AI ecosystem
integration. Maintain Buy (on CL).
Key takeaways
1. AI server and high-speed interconnect solution: Hon Hai showcased its AI

servers and interconnect solution in various architecture, including Vera Rubin
NVL72 rack-level AI servers, HGX Rubin NVL8 AI severs, Groq 3 LPX AI servers, and
the Spectrum-X SN6600 Ethernet switch, demonstrating the company’s
manufacturing and system integration capabilities. Management is positive on the AI
servers ramp up on the growing Gen-AI penetration and rising AI inferencing
demand. Besides the full system, the company also demonstrated the AI server

components including thermal components (3D VC, manifold), optical components
(ELSFP , optical modules) etc., reflecting its vertical integration.
2. AI Infrastructure solution in Europe: Hon Hai announced a strategic partnership
with leading AI computing supplier Bull to build the AI and Cloud infrastructure from
Europe to the global market. The AI deployment in France is expected to cost over
EUR 120m initially. We are positive on company’s global manufacturing scale and

supply chain capabilities to support Sovereign AI projects. Meanwhile, Hon Hai has
also partnered with Radiall and Thales on a plan to produce “made in Europe”
components with large scale (Link).

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Airtac International Group (1590.TW): May Monthly revenue -2% vs.
GSe; New orders continue to outpace shipments
3 June 2026 | 9:25PM CST
On June 2nd, AirTAC reported May revenue of Rmb844mn (-10% MoM, +26% YoY)
/ NT$3,909mn (-10% MoM, +40% YoY), which was 2% above GSe in Rmb terms.
In May, AirTAC’s revenue was 2% below GSe in Rmb terms, down 10% MoM with 9%
fewer working days, mainly due to the May holidays in Mainland China. This miss was

primarily due to (1) fewer working days MoM (20.5 days in May 2026 vs. 22.5 days in
April 2026), (2) electronics and battery showing double digit MoM decline, outpace
the actual decline in workings days, and (3) weaker than expected linear guide
revenue contribution (Rmb54mn missed GSe of Rmb62mn by 13%).
Moreover, May revenue was up 26% YoY (in Rmb) with the same working days as May
2025. We believe this solid YoY revenue growth was driven by (1) strong battery

demand, which continued to deliver strong YoY revenue growth over the past few
quarters, (2) YoY growth across nearly all end markets reached double digit YoY as
China pneumatic demand environment continue to improve. However, revenue
contribution from linear guides was lower in May at Rmb54mn vs. GSe of Rmb62mn
vs. Rmb60mn in April, but the daily average linear guides revenue remains high at
Rmb2.6mn (vs. Rmb2.7mn in April), as AirTAC’s linear guide products maintained

strong price competitiveness (c.20-30% discount vs. peers) and the company
expects the linear guide utilization rate to trend up sequentially throughout 2026
(see here
).
In terms of revenue growth by end-market segment for March (see Exhibit 1), the
battery sector continued to outgrow all other sectors (+55% YoY), suggesting
sustained demand. Key segments’ monthly revenue YoY trend includes textile (+51%
YoY), machine tool (35% YoY), general machinery (35% YoY), packaging (+20%),

Our Asia Industrial team highlighted strengthening demand across FA industry (see

supporting a high dividend payout ratio (50%-60%+ per year per GSe) in the foreseeable
future.
Maintain Buy on AirTAC with an unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,725.

Investment Thesis - AirTAC
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 25% market share
in 2024, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast
its market share will reach 31% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner
in a defla tionary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC

continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 25-30% OPM) and
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure
and financial health: with bes
t-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring.

Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long t erm given its
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC.
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,725 is based on a target P/E of 24.2x on

our 3Q26E-2Q27E EPS (avg of the last pneumatic upcycle).
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in
mainland China.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y
ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

by Goldman Sachs Global Investment Research and referred to in this research.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

• RTX Spark: Our checks suggest the RTX Spark NB targets to start shipping
in fall 2026, with ASP of >US$3k and combined shipments of N1 and N1X
"targeted" for  5-10mn in the first 12 months.
• "New" QD: Our checks suggest NVIDIA is looking to introduce a new design

for QDs used in the Rubin Ultra rack systems.
• Midplane connector: Based on our checks, FIT also got licensed for Paladin
HD2 connectors for midplane and will likely be the key partner for the next-
gen connector, though details around its designs have not been finalized.
• BlueField®-4 STX storage rack:
One tray contains 2 Vera, 4 BF-4 and 24
SSDs, in 2U form factor. There will be 16 trays in one STX rack; each tray
should consume 1.2-1.3kW. Our checks also suggest 1 STX rack will be paired

with 8 VR200 NVL72 racks.
• Spectrum X CPO switch:
Wistron showcased a Spectrum X CPO switch in
collaboration with NVIDIA. The CPO switch board PCB might use M6 CCLs
only.
• ELSFP:
FIT showcases its ELSFP products  in 20dBm (decibel-
milliwatts)/100mW (milliwatts) and 23dBm/200mW. Our checks suggest the
ASP of a 20dBm ELSFP could be close to ~US$500.

Key Takeaways
We don't think Kyber will be ready for mass deployment in Rubin Ultra.
All R200 HGX servers will be liquid-cooled, in 2U  form factor. Each server rack
will be able to house 9 nodes at most.
Groq 3 LPX rack: One tray contains 1 Intel CPU, 2 FPGA, 16 LPX and 28 QDs, with
double-sided main PCB,  which is 52L (26+26).
Vera CPU rack: A NVIDIA MGX Vera CPU board contains 2 Vera and 1.5TB

SOCAMM memory  (96GB x16) per board. Each tray houses 4 CPU boards (so 8
Vera per tray).
800V DC power rack: Our checks suggest the standalone 800V DC power rack is
on track to ship in 4Q26.

Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
Pegatron (4938.TW): Computex 2026: AI server in expansion; AI
infrastructure product portfolio showcased
4 June 2026 | 9:13PM HKT
We visited Pegatron’s booth on June 2 at our Computex tour in Taipei. Pegatron
showcased its AI infrastructure across cloud and edge AI, as well as quadruped
robot. Management remains positive on their AI server business growth and expects
continuous growth in coming years.
Key takeaways: Pegatron showcased its complete next-generation AI infrastructure

product portfolio at Computex (link), such as Nvidia Vera Rubin NVL72, Nvidia HGX
Rubin NVL8, and AMD GPU servers, focusing on the deployment of AI Factory,
High-Performance Computing (HPC), and AI data center. Pegatron’s 400G, 800G, and
1.6T switch were also showcased to fulfill the high-speed and lo w latency data
transmission requirements of AI data center. Management sees the growth
opportunities in AI infrastructure and expects a continuous expansion for its AI

server business in coming years, supported by the company’s transformation and
expansion from consumer electronics to AI servers. Besides AI infrastructures,
Pegatron also launched its next generation SIMBA II quadruped robot platform,
capturing the growth opportunities in edge AI computing and supporting the
company’s revenue growth ahead.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Pegatron
We are Sell rated on Pegatron with a 12-month target price of NT$66.2. Our TP is based
on a 10.0x 2027E P/E, which is derived from PC/server peers’ EPS growth vs. P/E multiple
correlation. We see EPS growth as a major factor for the stock’s share performance.
Key risks: 1) stronger-than-expected smartphone market recovery, 2)
faster-than-expected AI server ramp-up, and 3) stronger-than-expected demand on

consumer electronics.

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a B uy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not C overed), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region ’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the r eturn across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment R eview Committee or

other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such s tocks.
Total return potential represents the upside or downside differ ential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all cov ered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership .

Asustek Computer Inc.  | Asia Pacific
Strong Preliminary April Profits
Asustek reported April 2026 profit numbers per TWSE regulatory requirements.
• April 2026 revenue was NT$84.9bn.
• April 2026 NP was NT$5,313mn (+283% y/y), which accounts for 42% and
50% of our and the Street's 2Q26 NP estimates, respectively.
•  NpM was 6.3%
Backdrop: On its 1Q26 earnings call in May (report), Asustek reported a 1Q results
beat and provided strong 2Q26 guidance for its PC (+10-15% q/q) and server

businesses (+50% q/q).
This aligns with Lenovo's comment that PC pull-ins will persist into 2Q (
report),
though Asustek's OpM guidance appeared to be overly conservative at only 4-5%,
vs, April NpM of 6.3%..

Key Takeaways
Asustek's April profits imply upside risks to both our 2Q estimates and the
Street's.
However, we hold to our view that the market will repay the pulled-forward
demand sometime in 2H...
...when PC prices rise more meaningfully for consumers, which will eventually
lead to demand destruction.
We also think this is priced in with the stock up 51%  over the past month (the

TAIEX is up 12% over the same period).
Within our Greater China PC OEM coverage, we have a relative preference for
Lenovo (EW) over Asustek (UW) and Acer (UW).

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lenovo (0992.HK)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.3% (beta
of 1.1, risk-free rate of 2.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 5.0%, and a terminal growth rate
of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Stronger PC demand recovery in China
n Better cost savings and improved product mix
n Lower-than-expected increase in memory price

Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Weaker PC demand recovery in China
n Deterioration in cost savings and product mix
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory price
Acer Inc. (2353.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We use RI model for Acer's valuation. Key assumptions
include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 0.9, equity risk premium of 10.2% and risk-free rate
of 0.5%), a 6.0% medium-term growth rate, and a 3% terminal growth rate.

Risks to Upside
n Stronger macro recovery than expected
n Share gains in the PC market
n Better-than-expected cost controls
n Lower-than-expected increase in memory cost
Risks to Downside
n Weaker macro recovery than expected
n Share loss in the PC market
n Worse-than-expected cost controls
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory cost
Asustek Computer Inc. (2357.TW)
Base case, sum of the parts:
We apply a 9x multiple to Asustek's 2026 brand profits. This is somewhat below the midpoint

of its historical band of 7-15x since 2009, which we view as fair because memory prices are
rising and will have a negative impact on margins.
We value its stakes in Pegatron and Advantech based on our price targets in proportion to
Asustek's holdings.

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Largan Precision  | Asia Pacific
May Revenue Up 43% YoY but
Down 14% MoM
In this report, we focus on Largan's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• May revenue was NT$4,593mn, up 43% YoY but down 14% MoM. 5M26
revenue was up 15% YoY.
• The company expects June shipments to decline MoM, mainly owing to
customer scheduling adjustments, despite relatively stable underlying
demand.
Our view:
• We believe Android smartphone shipments will face headwinds in 2026,

which could constrain lens demand.
• The stock has re-rated significantly in recent weeks, mainly based on positive
CPO sentiment. However, after recognizing the challenges of scalable
production (high precision + huge workforce requirement), we believe
sentiment will calm down.
• P/E is currently  24x for 2026e,  at the high end of its valuation range of 13-
25x  over the past 12 months.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Largan Precision (3008.TW)
Base case, derived from a residual income model, the same valuation methodology that we
use for Greater China Hardware Tech companies in our coverage. Our key assumptions include
a cost of equity of 8.5%, a net profit CAGR in 2026-2036E of 7%, and a terminal growth rate
of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected high-end smartphone demand
n Faster-than-expected penetration of 1G6P

n Faster-than-expected penetration of periscopes
Risks to Downside
n Greater-than-expected competition, which would erode Largan's ASP and gross margin
n Weaker-than-expected smartphone demand, especially for high-end models
n Market share gains and more pricing pressure from Sunny

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

### Page 1

M
Update
Bizlink  | Asia Pacific
May Sales  Flat MoM/+41% YoY
In this report, we focus on Bizlink's monthly sales, which we
believe could be a catalyst for its share price.
Details:
• May sales came in at NT$7,352mn (flat MoM/+41% YoY).
• QTD reached 65% of both our 2Q26 estimate of NT$22,836mn (+9% QoQ/
+35% YoY) and consensus at NT$22,832mn (+9% QoQ/+35% YoY).
Our view:
• 2Q26 revenue tracking in line with market  expectations, in our view.
• We believe 1Q26 GM of 28.8% (-3.2ppt QoQ) should prove temporary, as the

impact of a less favorable HPC revenue mix should ease in the coming
quarters amid the new model ramp.
• Power interconnect products should benefit from server rack shipment
growth in 2026, while higher dollar content from next-generation GPU
servers and HVDC architecture should emerge mainly from 2027.
• Data interconnect products should continue to grow on wider AEC adoption
into 2026–27, with potential incremental contribution from optical solutions

(e.g., shuffle box, ALC) from 2027.
• Power interconnects should benefit from GB rack ramp-up this year, along
with higher dollar content from Rubin racks from 2026 and standalone
HVDC power racks from 2027, while data interconnects should continue to
grow on broader AEC adoption into 2026–27.
• We remain constructive on Bizlink as a beneficiary of AI, driven by data/
power interconnect and semi cap equipment businesses. Stay OW.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Bizlink (3665.TW)
Base case, derived from 30x 2027e P/E. We think our target P/E multiple is justified by an
earnings CAGR of 46% seen over 2025-28e, which implies a PEG of around 0.7. This is in-line
with the average of 0.7 in 2019-24, when the stock was trading at an average 13x P/E for a 19%
earnings CAGR.
Risks to Upside
n Faster and wider AEC adoption
n Data center and semi equipment capex increases that drive stronger HPC and semi-cap

business
n Stronger recovery for automotive business
Risks to Downside
n Lower-than-expected AEC adoption
n Intensifying competition
n Softer-than-expected AI capex
n Slower-than-expected growth in the semi capital equipment market
n Continued weakness in the auto industry

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

Gold Circuit Electronics Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
May sales up 20% m/m and
87% y/y
Upside to May numbers implies upside to 2Q: May revenue continues to reflect
adjusted pricing passed through to end customers in recent months. GCE’s
execution remains strong, and we like its exposure to high-end networking and ASIC
server PCBs. We expect continued mix improvement, and with additional capacity
coming online, the company should sustain growth in both top line and bottom line.

With Apr–May revenue now accounting for close to 70% of our and consensus
estimates, June revenue needs to be only ~NT$7.1bn (-19% MoM), which should be
readily achievable.
Tranium 3 UBB PCB ramping from late 2Q:
This schedule remains unchanged, and
we expect monthly revenue to start to reflect this from next month, with profit
improvement in 3Q.
Stay OW:  The stock is currently trading at ~16.5x/12.5x 2027/28 P/E, which we view
as attractive vs. its three-year forward P/E range of 7-25x and its Chinese peers at

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Gold Circuit Electronics Ltd. (2368.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of
1.0, equity risk premium of 8.7% and risk-free rate of 1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 12%,
and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected server and switch demand
n Stronger-than-expected NB demand
n Higher-than-expected pricing supported by strong product capability and market com-

petitiveness
n Faster-than-expected AI server penetration
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected server demand
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n More severe pricing erosion driven by competition
n Slower-than-expected AI server penetration

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

Update
WinWay Technology Co Ltd  | Asia Pacific
May sales came in better than
feared; OW
What's new? Winway reported May sales of NT$1,073mn, +8.5%M/M and +120%Y/
Y, better than buyside expectations of flat M/M. This was mainly helped by the
gradual ramp of new socket capacity and strong demand  from NVIDIA AI GPU/CPU,
AMD CPU, and Google TPU, although probe card contribution was lower.
Stay OW: We continue to expect 2Q revenue growth of 15-20% Q/Q, implying

strong June sales of +40% M/M, as new socket capacity comes fully  online to
support the strong backlog and incremental MEMS probe card revenue. The share
price has pulled back  amid investor concerns over weak May sales, which we see as
a good entry point  at 42x 2026e EPS.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
WinWay Technology Co Ltd (6515.TW)
We derive our price target from a residual income model, as we believe this can best capture
long-term value. We assume a 9.14% cost of equity (risk-free rate 2.0% and risk premium
5.5%), a payout ratio of 80%, a medium-term growth rate of 16.4%, and a terminal growth rate
of 4.5%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected adoption of hypersockets and MEMS probe cards

n Faster-than-expected SL T and burn-in socket introduction
n Stronger-than-expected edge AI demand
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected adoption of hypersockets and MEMS probe cards
n Slower-than-expected SL T and burn-in socket introduction
n No edge AI demand in the next few years

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Goldman Sachs (Asia) L.L.C.
Taiwan Technology: TSMT (6278.TW, NC): Mgmt. Call takeaways: SMT
solution expanding to AI optical module from consumer electronics
10 June 2026 | 10:52PM HKT
We talked to TSMT (Taiwan Surface Mounting Technology, 6278.TW, Not Covered)
management recently. TSMT is a global leading SMT (surface-mount technology)
solution provider that serves leading players in consumer electronics, automobile, AI
server ODM, optical networking, satellite, robotics, etc. Management highlights the

growth opportunities in AI infrastructures, such as optical modules and AI servers.
Management expects a rising contribution from optical module SMT business in
coming years, which carries a higher GM than consumer electronics, driving the
company’s earnings growth.
Read across to AI server supply chain: Management’s positive tone on AI
infrastructure supporting TSMT’s growth echoes our positive view on AI server
supply chain. We expect the global cloud capex uptrend and continuous AI

application development to support AI infrastructure end demand, and the
customization / diversifica tion trend (e.g. HGX, ASIC, AI servers powered by multiple
chipset suppliers) to continue, benefiting AI ser ver supply chain. Buy: Hon Hai,
Wiwynn, Mitac, Wistron, Lenovo.
Company profile: Taiwan Surface Mounting Technology (6278.TW, Not Covered) is a
global leading LCD SMT solution provider, providing material procurement, process
engineering design, SMT, quality assurance, etc. The company has a global layout,

with production sites in Taiwan, mainland China, India, Mexico, and Vietnam, and
serves leading players in consumer electronics, automobile, AI server ODM, optical
networking, satellite, etc.
Key takeaways: Management remains positive on growth ahead: (1) existing
business (consumer electronics SMT) to be supported by its major customer’s (a
US-based global leading brand) new notebook model, for which TSMT remains the
major supplier, (2) new business (AI infrastructure): TSMT penetrates a US-based

global leading optical module suppliers, and targets to further expand customer
base in optical modules. Customers’ products migration toward 1.6T optical modules
also drives TSMT’s growth ahead. The SMT for optical module also carries a higher
GM than consumer electronics, per management, supporting the company’s
profitability impr
ovement. AI servers is also another growing opportunity for the
company, per management, riding on the strong end demand growth driven by the

GS Factor Profile
The Goldman Sachs Factor Profile provides investment context for a stock by comparing key attributes to the market (i.e. our universe of rated stocks)
and its sector peers. The four key attributes depicted are: Growth, Financial Returns, Multiple (e.g. valuation) and Integrated (a c omposite of Growth,
Financial Returns and Multiple). Growth, Financial Returns and Multiple are calculated by using normalized ranks for specific metrics f or each stock. The

normalized ranks for the metrics are then averaged and converted into percentiles for the relevant attribute. The precise calcula tion of each metric may
vary depending on the fiscal y ear, industry and region, but the standard approach is as follows:
Growth is based on a stock’s forward-looking sales growth, EBITDA growth and EPS growth (for financial s tocks, only EPS and sales growth), with a
higher percentile indicating a higher growth company. Financial Returns is based on a stock’s forward-looking ROE, ROCE and CROCI (for financial

stocks, only ROE), with a higher percentile indicating a company with higher financial r eturns. Multiple is based on a stock’s forward-looking P/E, P/B,
price/dividend (P/D), EV/EBITDA, EV/FCF and EV/Debt Adjusted Cash Flow (DACF) (for financial s tocks, only P/E, P/B and P/D), with a higher percentile
indicating a stock trading at a higher multiple. The Integrated percentile is calculated as the average of the Growth percentile, Financial Returns

percentile and (100% - Multiple percentile).
Financial Returns and Multiple use the Goldman Sachs analyst forecasts at the fiscal y ear-end at least three quarters in the future. Growth uses inputs
for the fiscal y ear at least seven quarters in the future compared with the year at least three quarters in the future (on a per-share basis for all metrics).
For a more detailed description of how we calculate the GS Factor Profile, please c ontact your GS representative.

M&A Rank
Across our global coverage, we examine stocks using an M&A framework, considering both qualitative factors and quantitative factors (which may vary
across sectors and regions) to incorporate the potential that certain companies could be acquired. We then assign a M&A rank as a means of sc oring
companies under our rated coverage from 1 to 3, with 1 representing high (30%-50%) probability of the company becoming an acquisition targ et, 2
representing medium (15%-30%) probability and 3 representing low (0%-15%) probability. For companies ranked 1 or 2, in line with our s tandard

departmental guidelines we incorporate an M&A component into our target price. M&A rank of 3 is considered immaterial and theref ore does not
factor into our price target, and may or may not be discussed in research.
Quantum
Quantum is Goldman Sachs’ proprietary database providing access to detailed financial statement histories, forecasts and ratios. It can be used for
in-depth analysis of a single company, or to make comparisons between companies in differ ent sectors and markets.

Update
Compal Electronics  | Asia Pacific
May sales down 2% m/m, but
up 22% Y/Y
Our view:
• Unaudited April + May sales are tracking slightly behind our and consensus
estimates for 2Q26, primarily due to slower PC and consumer electronics
shipments.
• May notebook shipments came in 5% below our estimate at 2.0M units
(+11% m/m, -17% y/y).
• Compal's guidance continues to be that its notebook shipments will grow in
the low-single-digits q/q and revenue will grow in the double-digits q/q in

2Q26.
• We  believe the continued pull-in of consumer electronic devices in 1H26 will
lead to a more sub-seasonal 2H26.
• On our estimates, Compal is trading at ~17x 2027e P/E, above its past five-
year P/E average of ~14x.
• We stay UW on Compal.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Compal Electronics (2324.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.1% (beta
1.3, equity risk premium 6.0%, and risk-free rate 1.5%), a medium-term growth rate of 5.0%,
and a long-term earnings growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected NB and smartphone demand
n Further share gains in iPad
n Margin expansion thanks to better scale

Risks to Downside
n Weaker-than-expected NB demand
n Margin contraction from sales shortfall and fierce price competition
n Weaker-than-expected iPad shipments

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

• May revenue is tracking below our estimate and roughly in-line with the

• We estimate Wistron's 2Q GB rack-equivalents shipments at ~4.2K (+4% q/
q), unchanged from our previous estimate.
• We remain OW on the stock.

Key Takeaways
May sales were NT$290B (+2% m/m, +39% y/y).

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

1 
2609 陽明 ( YMTC )

報告日期：2026 / 06 / 02 研 究 員：林奕頎(Yichi Lin) 
壹、投 資 建 議 
一.美伊各懷鬼胎，拉扯協議，以拖待變，油價降，運價漲 
2 月 28 日美伊戰爭爆發，海運燃油（新加坡）從 2 月 27 日每
噸 697 美元，狂飆至 4 月 2 日每噸 1,789 美元，上漲 157%。 
後續，美伊進行停火協商，原油市場恐慌降溫，油價回跌。 
5 月 29 日最新報價，海運燃油每噸 1,003 美元，較美伊戰爭爆
發前的 2 月 27 日 697 美元，上漲 44%。 
同期間，航商因應市場恐慌（擔心經濟衰退） ，進行大幅減班，
更為轉嫁海運燃油成本，大幅調漲運價，SCFI 運價指數，從 2
月 27 日 1,333.11 一路走升至 6 月 1 日 2,571.73，上漲 93%。 
先前，油價暴漲，讓貨櫃航商驚慌，緊緊抱團，落實減班，大
幅推升運價 ， 超額轉嫁海運燃油成本 ， 反成為美伊戰爭受惠者。 
二.估 2Q / 2026 稅後 EPS + 1.15 元，YoY + 306%，QoQ + 179% 
1Q / 2026 單季 SCFI 運價指數平均 1,491 。 
2Q / 2026 單季 SCFI 運價指數（至 6 月 1 日）平均 1,988 。 
1Q / 2026 單季海運燃油平均每噸 840 美元。 
2Q / 2026 單季海運燃油（至 5 月 29 日） 平均每噸 1,228 美元。 
附註：5 月 29 日 SCFI 指數 2,571 ； 海運燃油 1,003 美元。 
宏遠預期 ， 受惠 季節性轉強 及 超額轉嫁燃油 ， 估計陽明2Q / 
2026 單季營收 QoQ + 75 億元； 燃油支出 QoQ + 29 億元； 推
升營業利益 QoQ + 38 億元； 單季稅後 EPS + 1.15 元，優於 1Q 
/ 2026 的 + 0.41 元，亦優於 2Q / 2025 同期的 + 0.28 元。 
三.短期間不易出現大規模軍事衝突，市場信心恢復，壓抑油價 
伊朗面對 經濟大幅衰退  及  石油停產 （毀壞油田）的壓力。 
美國面對 戰爭權力決議  6 0天 限 制  及  11 月 3 日期中大選。 
《戰爭權力決議》規定，若總統未獲國會授權派兵作戰，
必須在 60 天內向國會請求授權。 川普 3 月 2 日向國會報
告，起算 60 天；宣稱 4 月 7 日已停戰，無須國會授權。 
美伊雙方各有壓力，都需喘息空間，因此，雙方以拖待變的心
態濃厚，短期間，不易爆發大規模軍事衝擊，有利於恢復市場
信心，壓抑短期間油價漲勢（長期則必須觀察庫存水準） 。 
四.買進：超額轉嫁燃油成本，SCFI 創 20 個月新高，獲利跳升 
貨櫃航商，面對恐慌，緊緊抱團，大幅拉高運價，超額轉嫁燃
油成本； 同時，季節性轉強，即將進入 3Q 旺季； 此外，美
伊雙方以拖待變，有利恢復市場信心，油價恐慌短期退燒； 宏
遠預期 貨櫃航商 2Q 及 3Q 獲利逐季走高； 建議買進陽明。
投資評等：買進（調升） 
目標價位：69 元 
0.70 X 2026 年底每股淨值

參考價位：54.9 元 
   0.56 X 2026 年底每股淨值 
   2026 / 06 / 01 收盤

2026 年稅後 EPS 預估值 
從 + 0.30 元 調升至 + 4.07 元

股價利多： 
(1) SCFI 運價指數創  20 個月新
高。 
(2) 運價上漲， 超額轉嫁燃油成
本，有利於推升航商獲利。 
(3) 美伊各有壓力，以拖待變，油
價恐慌短期退燒。 
(4) 貨櫃航運季節性轉強，即 將進
入 3Q 旺季 ， 有利於支撐運價 。

貳、公 司 概 況 
一.公司簡介 
陽明，全球第 9 大貨櫃航商，以遠洋貨櫃航線 （歐洲線；美西線；
美東線）為主，以近洋航線（亞洲線）為輔。 
2025 年營收比重（依據 2026 年 3 月 25 日法說簡報）： 
歐洲線 38%； 美洲線 36%； 亞洲線 12%； 
其他 14%（中東、南美、澳洲）。

目前船隊（截至 2026 年 6 月 1 日；依據 Alphaliner 統計資料）： 
總營運艘數 97 艘，總營運 TEU 數為 740,200 TEU ，占全球貨櫃船
隊運力 2.2%： 自有：62 艘，總 TEU 數 366,696 TEU。 
租賃：35 艘，總 TEU 數 373,504 TEU。

訂船數量：待交船數 16 艘，合計 205,960 TEU， 
約當目前船隊運力 27.8%。

二.營運概況及展望 
1.美伊戰爭爆發，航商抱團拉抬運價，超額轉嫁海運燃油成本 
依照陽明海運 2026 年 3 月 25 日法說簡報 ， 海運燃油佔2025 年合
併營業成本 17% 。

依照  陽明 2025 年的成本結構，假設其他條件不變，則  平均油
價  每上漲  10% ，平均運價僅需上漲  1.5% ，即可超額轉嫁燃油
上漲成本 。

1Q / 2026 單季海運燃油平均每噸  840 美元。  
2Q / 2026 單季海運燃油（ 至 5 月 29 日）平均每噸  1,228 美元。  
2Q / 2026 （至 5 月 29 日），單季海運燃油 QoQ + 46% 。

1Q / 2026 單季 SCFI 運價指數平均  1,491 。 
2Q / 2026 單季 SCFI 運價指數（ 至 6 月 1 日）平均  1,988 。 
2Q / 2026 單季 SCFI （至 6 月 1 日）運價指數 QoQ + 33% 。

全球第 9 大貨櫃航商

(1) 季節性轉強，需求上升。 
(2) 產業恐慌，落實減班，大
幅拉升運價。 
(3) 美伊雙方以拖待變，油價
恐慌短期退燒。

貨櫃航商超額轉嫁燃油成本 
預期將推升 2Q 及 3Q 獲利

(1)4Q 貨櫃航運季節性轉弱 
(2)11 月美國期中選舉結束 
貨櫃航運可能再度大幅下滑

在 10% 油價漲幅，僅需  1.5% 運價漲幅  即可轉嫁  的情況下：  
航商拉抬 2Q / 2026 單季 SCFI 運價指數 QoQ + 33% ，即使假設半
數為固定價格的長約，使得整體運價（現貨  + 長約）實際漲幅
縮減為  16.5% ，這亦足夠轉嫁漲幅超過  110% 的燃油上漲成本；  
但 2Q / 2026 海運燃油價格 QoQ + 46% ； 因此，可以合理推估，
貨櫃航商已超額轉嫁燃油上漲成本，貨櫃航商 2Q 獲利將明顯優
於 1Q。

此外，  依據最新報價，貨櫃航商超額轉嫁的情況更為嚴重 ： 
6 月 01 日 SCFI 指數  2,571  ； 高於 2Q / 2026 單季平均  1,988 。 
5 月 29 日 海運燃油  1,003  ； 低於 2Q / 2026 單季平均 1,228 。

最新資料，顯示， 運價持續上漲，海運燃油持續下跌，貨櫃航商
超額轉嫁的幅度不斷擴大 ；因此，可以合理預期貨櫃航商 2Q / 
2026 獲利將明顯優於 1Q / 2026 。

宏遠估計，陽明 2026 年： 
營收 1,775.30 億元，YoY + 9%； 
陽明 公告 2026 年 1Q 財報營收YoY – 15% 。 
陽明公告 2026 年4月 單月營收 YoY + 14% ； 
創最近 9 個月新高。

營業利益 142.95 億元，YoY - 3%； 
(1) 預期 2026 年海運燃油平均價格上漲超過 7 成。 
(2)航商抱團減班，拉高運價，轉嫁燃油上漲成本。

即使運價拉升，但考量 1Q / 2026 財報營業利益 YoY 大幅
滑落； 加上，2026 年燃油成本大幅高於 2025 年； 因此，

業外淨收支 + 43.79 億元，YoY - 48% ； 
(1)連年發放高額現金股利，造成利息收入減少。

2026 年預計發放 2025 年每股現金股利 + 2 元，持續壓
抑 2026 年利息收入。 
利息收入差異，成為 2026 年業外淨收支 YoY 大幅下滑
的主因 。

(2) 2025 年認列 匯兌利益 11.28 億元 ， 成為 2026 年比較的
高基期。

(3) 2025 年認列 投資性不動產評價利益 4.30 億元 ，成為
2026 年比較的高基期。

稅後利益 + 142.05 億元，YoY - 17% ； 預估稅率 22.67%。

麗清 (3346 TT)  Buy

切入機器人供應鏈，投資建議維持 Buy，調升目標價。  
投資建議 Buy：雖 2026 年營運仍處於損平邊緣，但考量公司車
燈產品後續在手訂單提升，且切入敏實集團機器人供應鏈，有
利於公司長期發展與評價提升，投資建議維持 Buy ，目標價調
高至 28 元 (以 PB 0.95 倍評價 )。  
麗清切入敏實集團機器人供應鏈 ：麗清近期宣布切入機器人產
業。公司藉由在車用領域累積的車規級模組設計與 PCBA 製造
能力，以技術同源的優勢切入敏實集團的機器人與機器狗供應
鏈。首批產品預計於 06/2026 開始小量出貨。麗清開發的核心
關節控制模組將應用於人形機器人的 26 個大關節，以協助完成
複雜肢體運動與精準控制；同時也導入四足機器狗的 12 個關鍵
關節，以滿足高頻動態反應與多環境適應需求。在供應鏈佈局
上，麗清與敏實集團及旗下關鍵零組件廠綠的諧波結盟，由麗
清生產模組交由敏實組裝整合，最終出貨予中國人形機器人指
標廠智元機器人。此機器人產品線的毛利率推估落在
15%~20% ，2027 年為量產元年，預估訂單首年銷售額約 1.79 億
元人民幣，後續將隨著機器人市場需求提升而逐步放大。  
預估 2026 年營運仍處於損平邊緣： 1Q26 營收 16.94 億元，QoQ-
23.04% 、YoY-17.32% 。因受中國市場內捲影響且營收規模下滑，
毛利率下降至 8.89% ，因營業費用處於高檔，本業、整體營運陷
入虧損， EPS -0.67 元。 2Q26 方面， 04/2026 營收 MoM 已小幅
回升， 05/2026 、 06/2026 營收有望維持穩定。預估 2Q26 營收
20.64 億元， QoQ+21.85% 、YoY-9.04% ，因營收規模相對回升且
產品組合較佳，毛利率有望回升至 11.45% ，營業利益 0.16 億
元。 2Q26 整體營運小幅獲利， EPS 0.08 元。 2026 年方面，北美
車燈控制器出貨維持穩定，而中國市場持續內捲但狀況有機會
改善。預估營收 81.83 億元， YoY-4.21% 。毛利率於 1Q26 落底
後， 2Q26 有望回升，預估 2026 年毛利率較 2025 年小幅提升，
若公司 2Q26~3Q26 費用管控得宜，預估本業、整體營運表現將
落於損平邊緣， EPS -0.25 元。

2 群益投顧 
  麗清 (3346 TT) ESG ：  
溫室氣體排放： (1) 對於因應氣候變遷或溫室氣體管理之策略，以減排、綠能
生產及環保達到永續經營目標。並以生產製程減少廢棄物產生、優先採購對
環境傷害最少之原物料、管控耗能設備及依操作許可從嚴格處理生產過程
所產生之廢棄物。 (2) 溫室氣體排放量減量目標，在節能方面，透過持續的監
控系統及生產單位自主管理，使生產單位的電力消耗日益降低，達到能源的
有效利用，以年整體用電密集度下降 5%及年整體排碳密集度下降 5%為目
標。(3) 溫室氣體排放量減量之預算與計畫， 1.更新生產機器及使用採集中生
產原則。優先新機台生產，擬裝置節能變頻模組為優先，以較高效率的產能
先排產，再使用次舊機台，以節約電力消耗。機台開動以預計產量排滿為主，
提高稼動率，集中在較高效率的機台生產，減少低效率生產，減少溫室氣體
排放。 2.辦公室節能政策，室內空調冷氣以 26°C 為原則。午休時段用餐後
即關燈，下班後則分區關閉電源。 (4) 產品或服務帶給客戶或消費者之減碳
效果， 1.持續尋找新綠能材料或能源機會。 2.對內外單位儘可能以電子表單
寄送，減少紙張消耗。  
專業 LED 車燈模組供應商：  
麗清 (3346 TT) 為專業 LED 車燈模組供應商，除了量產 OEM LED 車燈模組
產品，也有能力為客戶設計打造 ODM 車燈模組產品。麗清為中國最大 LED
車燈模組廠，市占率接近三成。麗清車燈模組供應整車前裝市場，客戶包括
整車廠及車燈廠。以主要客戶占 2025 年營收比重來看，華域視覺 (車燈模組
與控制器 )占 34%、 HASCO( 控制器，華域視覺關係企業 )占 24%、海拉 (車燈
模組 )占 12% 、廣州小糸 (車燈模組，日系車為主 )占 9%、大茂 (車燈模組 )占
5%、 MAGNA( 車燈模組 )占 5%、 ZKW( 車燈模組，韓系 )占 3%、其他占 8%。
產品組合方面，車燈控制器 (包含出貨至美國及中國 )占 2025 年營收比重
29%、尾燈占 39%、頭燈及日行燈 (兩個會設計在一起 )占 31%、其他占 1%。

3 群益投顧 
產能擴充方面，因面臨中美貿易戰之影響且為了拓展海外市場，麗清於墨西
哥、泰國建立產能。麗清與當地台商合作，對方出場地及員工，麗清負責設
備、品保人員及接單、售後服務。泰國廠已於 2Q25 開始出貨；墨西哥廠預
計於 2Q26 開始出貨，且 04/2026 已接獲韓系車廠訂單 (此訂單預計 2027 年
開始出貨 )，以接單量來看，墨西哥廠營運規模將相對較大。  
圖二、麗清海外版圖擴點規劃

資料來源：麗清，群益投顧彙整  
麗清切入敏實集團機器人供應鏈：  
麗清近期宣布切入機器人產業。公司藉由在車用領域累積的車規級模組設
計與 PCBA 製造能力，以技術同源的優勢切入敏實集團的機器人與機器狗
供應鏈。首批產品預計於 06/2026 開始小量出貨。麗清開發的核心關節控制
模組將應用於人形機器人的 26 個大關節，以協助完成複雜肢體運動與精準
控制；同時也導入四足機器狗的 12 個關鍵關節，以滿足高頻動態反應與多
環境適應需求。在供應鏈佈局上，麗清與敏實集團及旗下關鍵零組件廠綠的
諧波結盟，由麗清生產模組交由敏實組裝整合，最終出貨予中國人形機器人
指標廠智元機器人 (Agibot) 。此機器人產品線的毛利率推估落在 15%~20% ，
2027 年為量產元年，預估訂單首年銷售額約 1.79 億元人民幣，後續將隨著
機器人市場需求提升而逐步放大。  
1Q26 營運陷入虧損，預估 2Q26 營運表現相對改善：  
1Q26 方面，營收 16.94 億元， QoQ-23.04% 、 YoY-17.32% 。因受中國市場內
捲影響且營收規模下滑，毛利率由 4Q25 的 9.67% 下降至 8.89% ，因營業費
用處於高檔，營業利益 -0.86 億元， QoQ 、 YoY 皆由盈轉虧。業外淨收入、
稅率皆相對下降，稅後純益 -0.81 億元， EPS -0.67 元。  
2Q26 方面， 04/2026 營收 6.87 億元， MoM+9.53% 、 YoY-15.08% ，營運已小

4 群益投顧 
幅回升， 05/2026 、06/2026 營收預估與 04/2026 相當。預估 2Q26 營收 20.64
億元， QoQ+21.85% 、YoY-9.04% ，因營收規模相對回升且產品組合較佳，毛
利率有望回升至 11.45% ，營業利益 0.16 億元。 2Q26 整體營運表現有機會
小幅獲利， EPS 0.08 元。  
預估 2026 年營運仍處於損平邊緣：  
2026 年方面，北美車燈控制器出貨維持穩定，而中國市場持續內捲但狀況
有機會改善。預估營收 81.83 億元， YoY-4.21% 。毛利率於 1Q26 落底後，
2Q26 毛利率有望回升，預估 2026 年毛利率較 2025 年小幅提升，若公司
2Q26~3Q26 費用管控得宜，預估本業、整體營運表現將落於損平邊緣， EPS 
-0.25 元。  
投資建議：  
投資建議方面，雖 2026 年營運仍處於損平邊緣，但考量公司車燈產品後續
在手訂單提升，且切入敏實集團機器人供應鏈，有利於公司長期發展與評價
提升，投資建議維持 Buy ，目標價調高至 28 元 (以 PB 0.95 倍評價 )。

  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌，亦將出具降評報告。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

訪談速報 
泰鼎-KY(4927) 
產品組合改善，有望於下半年損平 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $74 
前次投資建議：無

報告日期：2026.06.02 研究員：黃涵筠

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/6/1 收盤價： 55.80 產品組合升級，即將轉虧為盈，給予「買進」評等

(1) 泰鼎營運結構谷底回升，將達損平，並迎來獲利成長期，
受益於：1)韓國 Nvidia PCB供應大廠 ISU 技轉，出貨至
記憶體、伺服器板，板層數從過去 10 層躍升至 20 層。
2)透過 AI 伺服器代工股王切入 CSP 大廠，長期往 AI 伺
服器發展，板層數有望往 40 層升級，帶動平均單價、毛
利率倍增。 3)泰國 PCB產能稀缺，台韓 PCB龍頭大廠
相繼包下泰鼎產能， 2026 年稼動率逐季回升，有望於
26Q4 達損平。4)Nvidia 切入 AI PC市場，帶動換機潮，
有利泰鼎 PC板動能。 
(2) 泰鼎與韓國 PCB大廠 ISU 跨國合資成立 ISU-APEX。其
客戶包含 NVIDIA、Intel、北美四大 CSP、Cisco、Arista
等。因 ISU 缺少泰國產能，故而將相關訂單轉移給泰鼎
承接。也因泰國 PCB 產能稀缺，台韓 PCB 龍頭大廠相
繼包下泰鼎產能，下半年泰鼎將陸續出貨記憶體、高階
PC、伺服器板訂單，未來將往 AI伺服器發展，板層數從
過去 10層躍升至 20層，長期往40層升級，體質將大幅
改善。2026 年稼動率逐季回升，有望於 26Q4 達損平。 
(3) 看好 AI應用落地，Nvidia推出 RTX Spark架構 PC運算
晶片，有望刺激消費者與商務人士換機升級需求 。泰鼎
長期深耕 PC領域，近年積極切入高階 PC，除了 ISU為
策略合作夥伴，與韓國記憶體大廠長年合作，將出貨
DDR5 記憶體模組板，並直接受惠於 AI PC 滲透率拉升
帶來的產品結構與毛利率優化。

2026 年及 2027 年營收預估為 123 億元及 172 億元，年增
6%及 40%，稅後淨利-13 億元及 8 億元，26Q4 轉虧為盈並
於 2027 年進入獲利成長期，估稅後 EPS -5 及 2.84 元，給
予「買進」投資評等，目標價74 元(26X2027(F)PER)，潛在
上漲空間 33%。

訪談重點

泰鼎-KY為台廠在泰國最大之 PCB廠 
泰鼎-KY於 2009 年 10 月 28日在英屬開曼群島設立，2010 年 2 月 3日登錄
興櫃，2011年 10月 18日轉上櫃，2015 年 9 月 8日轉上市，總部位於泰國，
並有五個生產工廠，為台商在泰國最大之印刷電路板廠，與韓國 AI 伺服器
PCB大廠 Isu Petasys(梨樹飛達)跨國合資成立 ISU-APEX，轉型 PC、伺服
器板廠。現階段板層數最高達 20層，應用領域包含電視、setup box、衛星
通訊設備及車用多媒體設備等。目前轉型往記憶體、PC、伺服器板發展，未
來有望技術 升級並出貨高達 40 層之 AI PCB 板。營收應用比重為家用
35.5%、PC 23.5%、通訊 13.5%、車用 27.5%。競爭對手以泰國 KCE 以主，
其他包含敬鵬、東山精密、Nippon Mektron、TTM Technologies等公司。

26Q1 虧損逐步收斂，26H2將達損平 
26Q1 營收 27.6 億元，季增 3%、年減 12%，毛利率-12.5%，季增 0.9 個百
分點、年減17.3個百分點，稅後淨利虧損8.2 億元，稅後EPS -3.1 元。2026
年四月自結營收 9.2 億元，稅後EPS -0.6 元，相比第一季每月約虧損 1 元，
虧損已大幅收斂。過去公司出貨以低板層數之 10 層以下為主，較易受到削
價競爭，且過去處於產品轉換期。營收應用比重為家用35.5%、PC 23.5%、
通訊 13.5%、車用 27.5%。其中 PC 產品從前兩年之 12%上升至 23.5%，受
惠於公司轉型往較高層數之產品，並歸類於 PC 產品項，預期未來 PC 產品
線成長帶動獲利回升。

泰鼎營運結構谷底回升，將達損平，並迎來獲利成長期 
受益於：1)韓國 Nvidia PCB供應大廠 ISU 技轉，出貨至記憶體、伺服器板，
板層數從過去 10層躍升至 20層。2)透過 AI伺服器代工股王切入 CSP大廠 ，
長期往 AI 伺服器發展，板層數有望往 40層發展，帶動平均單價、毛利率倍
增。 3)泰國 PCB 產能稀缺，台韓 PCB 龍頭大廠相繼包下泰鼎產能，2026
年稼動率逐季回升，有望於 26Q4 達損平。4)Nvidia 切入 AI PC 市場，帶動
換機潮，有利泰鼎 PC板動能。

### Page 3

評等分級：買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅超過+30%）; 逢低買進（預期未來六個月股價漲幅介於+15%與+30%之間）; 區間操
作（預期未來六個月股價漲跌幅介於-15%與+15%之間）; 逢高賣出（預期未來六個月股價跌幅介於-15%與-30%之間）；賣出（預
期未來六個月股價跌幅超過-30%）。

- 3 - 
訪談速報 
AI PCB 大廠 ISU技轉，泰鼎成功轉型伺服器、記憶體 
泰鼎-KY與韓國伺服器及網通PCB大廠Isu Petasys(梨樹飛達)跨國合資成立
ISU-APEX。泰鼎出資 1,530 萬泰銖持股 15%，Isu Petasys 出資 8,670 萬泰
銖持股 85%。Isu Petasys 客戶涵蓋全球頂尖的雲端與 AI晶片巨頭，主要客
戶包含 NVIDIA、Intel、北美四大 CSP、網通與交換器霸主 Cisco、Arista、
Celestica等。因 Isu Petasys 生產重心在韓國、中國，缺少泰國產能，而主
要國際大型客戶皆偏好具有完整 PCB供應鏈之泰國產能 ， 故而駐場於泰鼎廠
房提供製程調教與良率優化，並將相關訂單轉移給泰鼎承接，以供應
「China+1」移出中國的訂單需求。也因泰國 PCB 產能稀缺，台韓 PCB 龍
頭大廠相繼包下泰鼎產能，並透過 AI 伺服器代工股王切入 CSP大廠。下半
年泰鼎將陸續出貨記憶體、高階 PC、伺服器板訂單，未來將往 AI伺服器發
展，板層數從過去10層躍升至 20層，長期往 40層發展，體質將大幅改善。
2026 年稼動率逐季回升，有望於 26Q4 達損平。

"A new era of PC", Nvidia切入 AI PC提高換機需求，有利泰鼎PC板動能 
看好 AI應用落地，最終邊緣AI將帶動 AI PC 升級潮，Nvidia以 Arm架構推
出 RTX Spark架構之 N1(主流款)、N1X(旗艦款)運算晶片，正式加入 Intel、
AMD、Apple 和 Qualcomm 的行列，切入 PC 處理器市場，全新 AI 運算架
構能處理更複雜的 AI任務，有望刺激消費者與商務人士換機升級需求，並有
利於 PC 板材供應鏈出貨動能。泰鼎長期深耕 PC 領域，近年積極切入高階
PC，除了 Isu Petasys 為策略合作夥伴，與韓國記憶體大廠長年合作，將出
貨 DDR5記憶體模組板，並直接受惠於AI PC 滲透率拉升帶來的產品結構與
毛利率優化。

產品組合改善，即將轉虧為盈，給予「買進」評等 
2026 年及 2027 年營收預估為 123 億元及 172 億元，年增6%及 40%，稅後
淨利-13億元及 8億元，26Q4 轉虧為盈並於 2027 年穩定獲利，稅後EPS -5
及 2.84 元。看好泰鼎受惠於：1)韓國 Nvidia PCB供應大廠 ISU 技轉，出貨
至記憶體、伺服器板，板層數從過去10層躍升至 20層。2)透過 AI伺服器代
工股王切入 CSP 大廠，長期往 AI 伺服器發展，板層數有望往 40 層升級，
帶動平均單價、毛利率倍增。 3)泰國 PCB產能稀缺，台韓PCB龍頭大廠相
繼包下泰鼎產能，2026 年稼動率逐季回升，有望於 26Q4 達損平。4)Nvidia
切入 AI PC 市場，帶動換機潮，有利泰鼎 PC 板動能。給予「買進」投資評
等，目標價 74元(26X2027(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 33%。

藥華藥 (6446 TT) 
雙軌獲利新賽道將啟動

永豐觀點 
ET 適應症美國地區取證在即，藥華雙軌獲利新賽道將啟動。 
 投資評價與建議 
建議買進：(1) 五月營收再創新猷，上修2Q 營收及獲利預估；(2) 3Q ET 適應症美
國地區取證在即，藥華雙軌獲利新賽道將啟動下，2026-27 強勁營運成長動能無
虞，維持買進，目標價因時序將進入下半年且獲利再上修，由948 元上調至 1,138
元( 32 X 2027 EPS )。

ESG 評析 
藥華企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 B+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

### Page 2

2

公司簡介：藥華生技為台灣新藥開發公司，以開發長效型蛋白質藥物為長，營運上
採研發、 生產到全球行銷一條龍模式， 目前除已在全球多國成功上市治療真性紅血
球增多症(PV)藥品 Besremi 外 ， 治療原發性血小板過多症(ET)美國等多市場也預計
2H26 取證，另治療早前期原發性骨髓纖維化(PMF)適應症三期臨床已啟動，整體
來看，除 ROPEG 新適應症持續拓展外，2026 年也有五項藥品將進入臨床一期。 
五 月 營 收 再 創 新 猷 ， 調 升2Q 營 收 及 獲 利 預 估：藥 華五月營收 24,6 億元
(+19.1%MoM、+108.5%YoY)， 累計四五月營收45.2 億元 ， 達2Q 預估營收 73%，
表現優於預期，主因 PV 用藥主力市場美國及日本等地用藥人數持續爬升，與美國
CMS 特定小型製造商資格所適用之藥價折扣優惠、於營收中認列部分相關調整金
額，上修 2Q 營收由 62.4 億元至 68.2 億元(+33.2%QoQ、+89.4%YoY)，毛利率
88.6%，稅後淨利 25.7 億元(+2.2%QoQ、+74.3%YoY)，稅後 EPS 6.58 元(原估
5.52 元)。 
3Q 美國地區 ET 取證在即 ， 啟動雙軌獲利新賽道 ：藥華目前營收來源主要為 Ropeg
新藥在 PV(真性紅血球增多症)治療上，美國、日本等市場用藥人數持續攀升，由於
美國地區 PV 注射筆劑型取證機率大，將大幅提升病患用藥便利性，加上公司今年
起亦積極將此藥往早期治療領域推廣下 ， 研究處預估PV 今年美國市場治療人數有
機會由去年約 3,000 人再爬升至 5,000 人，另治療原發性血小板過多症第二適應
症 ET，美國市場預計八月底取證，因此藥在SURPASS-ET 部分已獲 NCCN 通知，
列為對現行治療反應不佳或無效之高風險 ET 病患的第一類首選療法，有助縮短上
市後之推廣與縮短保險給付審核時間外 ，EXCEED-ET 試驗結果與 SURPASS-ET 相
當 ， 且在未接受過治療的患者中展現更優異的療效， 預期上市後應有機會快速拓展
ET 全族群治療，加上 ET 與 PV 同屬骨髓增生性腫瘤領域，對藥華而言，上市後無
需再重新建立銷售渠道與醫生溝通下，預估 ET 取證上市後營收爬升速度將甚於
PV，2026 及 2027 年營收在兩藥同步貢獻下可達 262.4 億元(+67.9%YoY)及 371.8
億元(+41.7%YoY)，動能強勁。 
財務預估 ：因 2Q 營收表現優於預期 ， 上修2026 年營收預估由 248.7 億元至 262.4
億元(+67.9%YoY)，毛利率 88.4%，稅後淨利 98.2 億元(+94.6%YoY)，稅後 EPS 
25.72 元(原估 23.17 元)，2027 年營收 371.8 億元(+41.7%YoY)，毛利率 88%，
稅後淨利 135.7 億元(+38.2%YoY)，稅後 EPS 35.55 元(原估 35.13 元)。

### Page 2
